INDUSTRY AND BUSINESS REVIEW                                               行業及業務回顧

During the year under review, dragged by the pandemic and the              回顧年內，受疫情拖累，內地消費意願下
sluggish consumer sentiment in the Mainland China, the demand for          降，包裝紙需求疲弱，價格持續偏低，但
packaging paper remained weak with price slipping lower, but the           紙漿等原材枓卻因俄羅斯烏克蘭戰爭帶來
prices for pulp and other raw materials surged as the result of the        的供需缺口而漲價，紙企盈利空間再被壓
Russo-Ukrainian War, which further squeezed the profitability of           縮。有見紙漿價格居高不下，部分紙企主
paper manufacturers. Under the pressure of high pulp prices, some          動停機減產。
of the paper manufacturers voluntarily suspended or reduced their
production.


In the face of changes in market supply and demand and policy              面對市場供需及政策變化，本集團積極拓
adjustments, the Group pro-actively expanded its pulp business and         展木漿業務，增強原材料配置，以減輕原
rationalized its raw material allocation in order to mitigate the impact   料成本上漲對盈利的影響。本集團亦相信
of rising raw material costs on our profitability. The Group also          消費需求久被壓抑後必定釋放，帶動造紙
believes that consumer demand is set to be unleashed after a long          業回暖，故堅持在現有業務基礎上擴充規
period of suppression, leading to a rebound in the paper                   模，包括在江西及重慶廠房新增生產線，
manufacturing industry. Therefore, the Group insisted on expanding         並計劃於廣西新建漿廠，力求透過提升產
the scale of production of its existing business, including the addition   能及優化控制成本，進一步增強經營韌
of new production lines at its plants in Jiangxi province and              性，為長遠發展奠下更好基礎。
Chongqing and the plan of building a new pulp mill in Guangxi
province, with a view to further enhancing the resilience of its
operations and laying a more solid foundation for its long-term
development by increasing production capacity and rationalizing cost
control.


In addition, the Group continued to support the “Belt and Road”            另外，本集團繼續響應「一帶一路」倡議，

BUSINESS PROSPECTS                                                        業務前景

After the PRC government optimized its anti-pandemic policy in last       內 地 自 去 年 12 月 優 化 防 疫 政 策 後 ， 各 地
December, measures to increase investment, boost consumption and          陸續推出擴大投資、提振消費、促進創新
promote innovation were introduced in various provinces so as to          的 措 施 ， 為 2023 年 經 濟 穩 中 求 進 提 供 支
support economic stability and progress in 2023. With the increase in     持。隨著工業和消費活動增加，造紙業景
industrial and consumer activities, the paper manufacturing industry is   氣度將有望恢復，其回升幅度相信在下半
expected to recover at a more promising pace in the second half of        年更見明顯，前景審慎樂觀。本集團將持
the year, leading to a cautiously optimistic outlook. The Group will      續擴充產業鏈，以實現紙張和紙漿業務多
continue to expand its industrial chain in order to diversify its paper   元化的策略，減輕原料成本上漲的壓力，
and pulp business so as to reduce the pressure of rising raw material     靈活應對市場對各產品的需求，進一步提
costs, respond flexibly to market demand for various products and         升營運效益。
further enhance our operational efficiency.


Packaging paper remains the Group’s core business. With the               包裝紙業務是本集團的核心業務。內地對
tightening control on plastic packaging material in the Mainland          塑料包裝的管控力度趨嚴，作為塑膠包
China, we see much of growth potential for packaging paper as an          裝的重要替代，紙質包裝仍有發展潛力，
excellent alternative to plastic packaging material. As the popularity    加上疫情帶動網購增長，在疫後促消費措
of online shopping grew during the pandemic, demand for                   施的推動下，包裝紙需求有望回升。本集
packaging paper is expected to pick up upon the implementation of         團將繼續擴大產能覆蓋面，以提升盈利能
the post-pandemic consumption stimulation measures. The Group             力。本集團正在江西、重慶及廣西廠增設
will continue to expand its coverage of the production capacity to        木 漿 生 產 線 ， 並 預 計 分 別 於 2023 年 第 二
enhance its profitability. The Group adds pulp production lines at its    季、第三季及第三季完成，新增木漿年產

Anti-bribery, Anti-fraud and Anti-money Laundering                            防止賄賂、欺詐及洗黑錢
The Group attaches great importance to integrity supervision, and is          本集團高度重視廉潔監管，致力防範貪
committed to preventing corruption, bribery, fraud and other                  腐、賄賂、欺詐等違法和違反商業道德的
behaviors that are illegal and against commercial ethics, to make sure        行為，確保營運全程遵守所有防止商業罪

commercial crimes in our daily operations, including but not limited          人 民 共 和 國 刑 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 反
to the “Criminal Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the “Anti-           洗 錢 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 反 不 正 當 競
Money Laundering Law of the People’s Republic of China”, the                  爭法》等。法規要求企業需遵循平等、公
“Anti-Unfair Competition Law of the People’s Republic of China”               平、誠信等原則，依法而行，堅守商業道
and so forth. Enterprises are required under laws and regulations to          德，不得以盜竊、賄賂、欺詐、脅迫或者
abide by the principles of fairness, equality and integrity, to act in a      以其他不正當手段經營。
lawful manner, to adhere to commercial ethics, and not to operate
business by means of theft, bribery, deception, compulsion or other
improper means.


The Group believes that to achieve anti-corruption, it relies the             本集團相信實現反貪污有賴多方內外合
cooperation of both internal and external parties within the                  作。因此，所有員工亦需要遵守本集團編
organization. Therefore, all employees are required to abide by the           製的《商業道德規範》及《反貪污政策》要求
“Standards for Commercial Ethics” and “Anti-Corruption Policy”                員工以專業合規、平等公正、誠信正直的
complied by the Group, which restricts employees to serve the                 行為服務公司和客戶。本集團的員工守則
Company and customers with professional compliance, equality,                 中亦明文規定了個人及專業操守以期嚴格
fairness, and integrity. The Worker’s Manual of the Group also clearly        制約、防範交易過程出現任何特殊待遇，
stipulates code of personal and professional conduct for their                包括但不限於回扣、昂貴禮品、奢侈款待
stringent compliance, pursuant to which employees are not allowed             等，以免影響其客觀專業的判斷。同時，
to accept any special privilege during transactions, including but not        本 集 團 員 工 在 入 職 時 均 須 簽 署《 廉 潔 從 業
limited to rebates, expensive gifts, luxury hospitality, etc., so as not to   承 諾 書 》， 承 諾 不 可 以 利 用 工 作 職 權 上 的
affect their objectivity and professional judgment. In addition, all          便利為自己索取任何好處，不得為牟取自
employees of the Group are required to sign “Undertaking to                   身利益而損害公司權益。

Diseases of the People’s Republic of China” and other relevant safety    監管，強調安全生產的重要性，保障員工

property. We shall take the objectives of “Safety First, with Emphasis   綱領，建立了完善的安全生產及職業病防

Labour Standards                                                           勞工準則

Prohibition of Child and Forced Labour                                     防止童工或強制勞工
The Group recruits qualified workers in adherence to the principles of     本集團按照公平、公開、自願的原則招聘
fairness, openness and free will. Every subsidiary of the Group            錄 用 合 資 格 的 員 工 。 各 個 子 公 司 根 據《 中
entered into legal employment contract with employees in                   華人民共和國勞動合同法》或當地適用勞
accordance with the “Labour Contract Law of the People’s Republic          工法與員工簽訂合法的僱傭合同，無強制
of China” or applicable local labour laws and there was no forced          勞 工 行 為 。 同 時 ， 本 集 團 嚴 格 按 照《 中 華
labour. In the meantime, the Group only recruits people who have           人民共和國勞動法》及其他適用的當地勞
attained the age of 16 in strict compliance with the “Labour Law of        工法要求，只招聘錄用已年滿16周歲的人
the People’s Republic of China” and other applicable local labour          士 。 本 集 團 內 部 設 立《 未 成 年 工 使 用 及 禁
laws and regulations. The Group formulated its own “Management             止使用童工管理規定》防止童工或不法勞
Rules for the Use of Minors as Labourers and Prohibition against the       工，保障未成年人的合法權益。人力資源
Use of Child Labour” to prevent the use of child or illegal labour and     部會在招聘流程中核對應聘者的個人資料
to protect the legitimate rights and interest of the minors. During the    並進行背景調查，本集團會保留任何調查
recruitment process, the human resources department would check            結果異常之個案的錄用登記及核查材料，
the personal information of applicants and conduct background              並 終 止 其 應 徵 過 程 。 本 集 團 亦 於《 員 工 手
investigation. If such investigation generated any irregular result, the   冊》中鼓勵員工舉報使用他人身份證明文
Group would retain the record and cease the application process            件的情況，從多方面達成管控目標。
accordingly. In the “Worker’s Manual“, the Group also encourages
employees to whistleblow on the use of false identity documents in
order to achieve effective control from different perspectives.

in China. Therefore, the operation of the Group is principally            生用紙工廠，因此本集團在經營業務時主

Environmental Protection”, the “Law of the People’s Republic of           保 護 法 》、《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 水 污 染 防 治
China on Prevention and Control of Water Pollution”, the “Law of          法》、《中華人民共和國大氣污染防治法》
the People’s Republic of China on Prevention and Control of Air           和《 中 華 人 民 共 和 國 固 體 廢 物 污 染 環 境 防

Prevention and Control of Environmental Pollution Due to Solid            標準所規管。
Wastes” in Mainland China and regulations and emission standards

In accordance with the requirements, environmental protection             據規管要求，企業的環保策略需以保護優
strategies adopted by enterprises shall follow a principle of “priority   先、預防為主、綜合治理、公眾參與、損
to protection, emphasis on prevention, integrated governance, public      害擔責為原則，以合規措施防治污染和危
participation and liability assumption of damages”, in order to           害，並建立環境保護責任制度，明確企業
prevent and control pollution and damages with compliance                 及相關負責人員的責任，期望在為各持份
measures, to establish an accountability system for environmental         者創造商業價值的同時，降低對環境的影
protection and to clarify responsibilities of enterprises and related     響。國家亦鼓勵企業採用先進及最新技術
responsible officers, hoping to create commercial value for all           處理污染物，推動環保發展。
stakeholders while reducing the impact on environment. The use of
advanced and latest pollutant treatment technologies are also
encouraged by China for facilitating the environmentally-responsible
development.

Being well aware that environmental protection is an important basis    本集團深明環境保護是可持續發展的重要
for its development, the Group has adopted a number of                  基礎，因此採取多項改進措施及監控方法

our environmental responsibilities. The Group has established various   的各項環保政策與措施包括：
environmental protection policies and measures including:

Setting Up Environmental Team
成立環保小組

Led by our Chief Executive Officer, the environmental team comprises members having the relevant expertise,
including senior flue gas treatment experts, sewage treatment technicians, environmental monitoring professionals
and environmental protection technicians so as to ensure effective implementation of the following measures.
由首席執行官親自帶領，由合資格專才組成，包括高級煙氣治理師、污水處理人員、環境監測人員、環境保護專業
技術人員等，保證下列相關環保措施得以有效執行。

Duties of Environmental Team
環保小組職責

1.   Holding internal control meetings regularly to review environmental issues in the plants, to update themselves
     with applicable environmental laws and regulations and to give advice on any improvements;
2.   On an ongoing basis, the team engages themselves in environmental protection measures to be taken in
     production processes, including those employed to lower water consumption, treat sewage water and air
     emissions more efficiently, save energy and monitoring emissions to ensure continued compliance with high
     environmental protection standards.
1.   定期召開內部監控會議，旨在審閱有關廠房內的環保事宜，通知各成員環保法律及規定的更新條文，並為存
     在改善空間的環節提供適當建議；
2.   不斷完善生產過程中的環保措施，包括減少耗水量、應用更有效的廢水處理技術、節省能源耗用量、採用更
     環保的廢氣處理技術及排放監控等，確保可持續地達到高環保標準。


Environmental Policies and Measures
環保政策與措施

✓    set up policies for emissions treatment to provide clear targets and state the responsibilities and division of
     labour for environmental protection measures adopted by the Group;
✓    formulate guidelines for energy-saving and reduce emission at the source;
✓    as part of the various efforts to make us one of the companies which best complies with environmental
     protection principles in the world, our different plants have employed technologies with high efficiency to
     reduce emission and keep our emission below the latest emission standards laid down by the government as
     well as internationally applicable environmental management standards.
✓    設立排放物處理辦法，為本集團採納之環保措施提供明確的目標和職責分工；
✓    訂立節約能源指引，從源頭減排；
✓    各廠房利用高效率技術降低排放，保持低於政府最新排放標準的排放水準，對標國際環境管理標準，並努力
     成為全球最符合環保原則的公司之一。

own thermal power stations operating coal-fired generators and            站通過燃煤發電機組發電，因而產生一定
therefore, there have been gas emissions including nitrogen oxides        的氣體排放，包括氮氧化物和硫氧化物。

Case Study: Environmentally friendly Thermal Power Station                個案研究：環保熱電站
Environmentally friendly circulating fluidised bed boilers used in our    本集團的熱電站一致採用環保型的循環流
thermal power stations can achieve efficient combustion of all kinds      化床鍋爐，可以有效焚燒各煤種，該種鍋
of coal. As the combustion temperature in this kind of boilers is lower   爐燃燒溫度低於煤粉爐，產生的氮氧化物
than that in pulverised coal boilers, less nitrogen oxides would be       排放較少。
generated.

systems to eliminate nitrogen oxides, and limestone-gypsum flue gas       灰石膏脫硫法去除二氧化硫等。目前煙塵
desulfurisation system to curb sulphur dioxide gas. We have               去 除 率 已 達 99.9% 以 上 ， 脫 硫 率 達 90%
successfully reduced smoke and sulphur by more than 99.9% and             以上。
90% respectively.

Cope with Climate Change                                                 氣候變化應對
Global warming is notably affecting the natural climate by increasing    全球變暖顯著影響自然氣候，使得極端天
the frequency and intensity of extreme weather and natural disasters     氣加劇，暴雨、洪水及乾旱等天災的頻
such as rainstorm, flood and drought, which greatly aggravated the       率和強度增加，大幅加劇了各類天災的破
damage and impact of various natural disasters. To cope with the         壞及影響程度。為應對越趨嚴重的氣候威
intensified threat of climate change, management of our Group has        脅，本集團的管理層定期評估生產經營中
regularly assessed the potential risks that may arise from cause         因氣候變化而可能導致的潛在風險，並採
climate change during production and operation and adopted               取預防及應變措施。
preventive and contingency measures.


We have strategically selected location for our plants and drawn up      我 們 策 略 性 地 為 廠 房 選 址 ， 制 定《 極 端 天
the “Extreme Weather Catastrophes Contingency Plan“. We classify         氣 災 害 應 急 預 案 》， 按 照 風 險 可 能 造 成 的
the extreme weather catastrophes into four degrees, namely very          危害程度，將極端天氣災害分為四個等
significant, significant, major and general according to the degree of   級：特別重大、重大、較大、一般。同
harm that may be caused by such risks. Meanwhile, we have                時，我們設立了清晰的應變體系，成立了
established a clear contingency system and set up the Steering           極端天氣應急救援領導小組，明文規定了
Committee on Extreme Weather Contingency and Rescue, which               各成員職能、預警行動和應急措施。我們
expressly stipulates functions of each member, alert actions and         亦會日常定期向員工進行宣傳、培訓和演
contingency measures. We arrange publicity, trainings and drills for     習，提高員工的警惕和應災能力，於颱
employees regularly, aiming at enhancing the vigilance and abilities     風、暴雨、雷電、冰雹、洪水等極端天氣
towards disasters. We will assess how bad we will be affected by the     出現時評估影響，做好相關安全措施並提
extreme weather such as hurricane, rainstorm, thunder, hail and          供安全場所讓員工暫避，保障員工生命安
flood so that we can take relevant safety measures and provide a         全並避免生產機械損失。同時，各廠房均
safe place for our employees. With these measures, we will safeguard     設有熱電站和後備電源，確保廠房持續穩
our employees’ safety and avoid losses arising from the malfunction      定的生產運作，降低因區域性停電而停產
of equipment. In addition, our plants have their own thermal power       的風險。
stations and backup power which are necessary for a continuous and
stable production and can reduce the risk of halt in production due

            the amendments clarify that:

                   management intentions or expectations to settle                          二個月內結清負債的意向
                   the liability within 12 months; and                                      或預期所影響；及


            (ii)   if the right is conditional on the compliance with                (ii)   倘該權利以遵守契諾為條
                   covenants, the right exists if the conditions are met                    件，即使貸款人在較後日
                   at the end of the reporting period, even if the lender                   期才測試是否符合條件，
                   does not test compliance until a later date; and                         該權利在報告期末符合條
                                                                                            件的情況下存在。

                                                                                         ）續）
   (“HKFRSs”) (CONTINUED)

     Amendments to HKAS 1 and HKFRS Practice Statement                       香港會計準則第1號及香港財務報告
     2 Disclosure of Accounting Policies                                                  會計政
                                                                             準則實務報告第2號（修訂本）
                                                                             策之披露
     HKAS 1 is amended to replace all instances of the term                  香 港 會 計 準 則 第 1 號 修 訂 以 取 代「 主
     “significant accounting policy information”. Accounting policy          要會計政策資料」一詞的所有實例。
     information is material if, when considered together with other         倘連同實體財務報表內其他資料一
     information included in an entity’s financial statements, it can        併考慮，會計政策資料可以合理預
     reasonably be expected to influence decisions that the primary          期會影響通用財務報表的主要使
     users of general purpose financial statements make on the               用者根據該等財務報表所作出的決
     basis of those financial statements.                                    定，則該會計政策資料屬重大。


     The amendments also clarify that accounting policy information          該等修訂亦澄清，即使涉及款項並
     may be material because of the nature of the related                    不重大，但基於相關交易性質、其
     transactions, other events or conditions, even if the amounts           他事項或情況，會計政策資料仍可
     are immaterial. However, not all accounting policy information          屬重大。然而，並非所有與重大交
     relating to material transactions, other events or conditions is        易、其他事項或情況有關的會計政
     itself material. If an entity chooses to disclose immaterial            策資料本身即屬重大。倘一間實體
     accounting policy information, such information must not                選擇披露非重大會計政策資料，有
     obscure material accounting policy information.                         關資料不得掩蓋重大會計政策資料。


     HKFRS Practice Statement 2 Making Materiality Judgements                香港財務報告準則實務報告第2號作
     (the “Practice Statement”) is also amended to illustrate how an         出 重 大 性 判 斷（「 實 務 報 告 」）亦 經 修
     entity applies the “four-step materiality process” to accounting        訂，以說明一間實體如何將「四步法
     policy disclosures and to judge whether information about an            評估重大性流程」應用於會計政策披
     accounting policy is material to its financial statements.              露及判斷有關一項會計政策的資料
     Guidance and examples are added to the Practice Statement.              對其財務報表是否屬重大。實務報
                                                                             告已增加指導意見及實例。


     The application of the amendments is not expected to have               應用該等修訂預期不會對本集團財
     significant impact on the financial position or performance of          務狀況或表現產生重大影響，但
     the Group but may affect the disclosures of the Group’s                 可能影響本集團主要會計政策之披
     significant accounting policies. The impacts of application, if         露 。 有 關 應 用 影 響（ 如 有 ）將 於 日 後
     any, will be disclosed in the Group’s future consolidated               本集團的綜合財務報表中予以披露。

      The amendments define accounting estimates as “monetary               該等修訂本將會計估計定義為「存在
      amounts in financial statements that are subject to                   計量不明朗因素的財務報表之貨幣

      involves measurement uncertainty – that is, the accounting            行計量─即會計政策可能規定將按

      estimated. In such a case, an entity develops an accounting           一間實體應編製會計估計，旨在達
      estimate to achieve the objective set out by the accounting           到會計政策載列的目標。編製會計
      policy. Developing accounting estimates involves the use of           估計包括使用根據最新可得可靠的
      judgements or assumptions based on the latest available,              資料作出的判斷或假設。
      reliable information.

      HKAS 8 is retained with additional clarifications.                    計變更的概念予以保留，並已作出

          Basis of consolidation (Continued)                                        （續）
                                                                               綜合賬目基準
          The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an                   倘事實及情況顯示上述三項控
          investee if facts and circumstances indicate that there are          制元素其中一項或多項改變，
          changes to one or more of the three elements of control              則本集團會重新評估其是否仍
          listed above.                                                        然控制被投資方。


          Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group                  當本集團取得附屬公司之控制
          obtains control over the subsidiary and ceases when the              權，便將該附屬公司綜合入
          Group loses control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income          賬； 當本集團失去附屬公司之
          and expenses of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of                 控制權，便停止將該附屬公司
          during the year are included in the consolidated                     綜合入賬。具體而言，年內收
          statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive                  購或出售之附屬公司之收入及
          income from the date the Group gains control until the               支出會於本集團取得控制權當
          date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                日起計入綜合損益及其他全面
                                                                               收益表，直至本集團對該附屬
                                                                               公司之控制權終止當日為止。


          Profit or loss and each component of other                           損益及其他全面收益的各個項
          comprehensive income are attributed to the owners of                 目乃歸屬於本公司擁有人及非
          the Company and to the non-controlling interests. Total              控股權益。即使會導致非控股
          comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed to the            權益出現虧絀結餘，附屬公司
          owners of the Company and to the non-controlling                     之總全面收益仍會歸屬於本公
          interests even if this results in the non-controlling                司擁有人及非控股權益。
          interests having a deficit balance.

          simplified assessment of whether an acquired set of                      化評估一項收購活動及資產是

          test is met if substantially all of the fair value of the gross          產總額的公允值基本上全部集
          assets acquired is concentrated in a single identifiable                 中在單個可識別資產或一組類
          asset or group of similar identifiable assets. The gross                 似可識別資產中，則符合集中
          assets under assessment exclude cash and cash                            度測試。受評估的總資產不包
          equivalents, deferred tax assets, and goodwill resulting                 括現金及現金等價物、遞延稅
          from the effects of deferred tax liabilities. If the                     項資產、及源自遞延稅項負債

          assessment is needed.                                                    並非業務及毋須作進一步評

     3.2 Significant accounting policies (Continued)                               3.2 主要會計政策
                                                                                            （續）

          Business combinations or asset acquisitions                                        （續）
                                                                                     業務合併或資產收購
          (Continued)
          Asset acquisitions                                                         資產收購
          When the Group acquires a group of assets and liabilities                  當本集團收購並不構成業務之
          that do not constitute a business, the Group identifies                    一組資產及負債，本集團假設
          and recognises the individual identifiable assets acquired                 首先將購買價格按各自公允值
          and liabilities assumed by allocating the purchase price                   分配給金融資產╱金融負債，
          first to financial assets/financial liabilities at the respective          購買價格餘額則按於購買當日
          fair values, the remaining balance of the purchase price is                其相關之公允值基準分配給其
          then allocated to the other identifiable assets and                        他可識別資產及負債，以識別
          liabilities on the basis of their relative fair values at the              及確認所收購之個別可識別資
          date of purchase. Such a transaction does not give rise to                 產及負債。這樣的交易不會產
          goodwill or bargain purchase gain.                                         生商譽或討價還價的購買收
                                                                                     益。


          Investment in an associate and a joint venture                             聯營公司及合營企業投資
          An associate is an entity over which the Group has                         聯營公司指本集團可對其施加
          significant influence. Significant influence is the power to               重大影響的實體。重大影響指
          participate in the financial and operating policy decisions                可參與被投資方之財務及營運
          of the investee but is not control or joint control over                   政策決定之權力，惟對該等政
          those policies.                                                            策並無控制權或共同控制權。


          A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties                 合營企業是一種共同安排，據
          that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to                  此共同控制該安排的各方擁有
          the net assets of the joint arrangement. Joint control is                  該共同安排的淨資產。共同控
          the contractually agreed sharing of control of an                          制為一項合約協定共享控制之
          arrangement, which exists only when decisions about the                    安排，僅會於決定相關事項須
          relevant activities require unanimous consent of the                       共享控制的各方一致同意時方
          parties sharing control.                                                   會出現。

          joint venture are incorporated in these consolidated                    資產與負債乃按權益會計法列
          financial statements using the equity method of                         入綜合財務報表內。按權益法
          accounting. The financial statements of an associate and                列賬的聯營公司及合營企業的
          a joint venture used for equity accounting purposes are                 財務報表乃採用本集團在類似
          prepared using uniform accounting policies as those of                  情況就類似交易及事件所採用
          the Group for like transactions and events in similar                   的劃一會計政策而編製。根據
          circumstances. Under the equity method, an investment                   權益法，聯營公司及合營企業
          in an associate and a joint venture is initially recognised in          投資於綜合財務狀況表初始按
          the consolidated statement of financial position at cost                成本確認，及其後調整以確認
          and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share of               本集團應佔該聯營公司及合營
          the profit or loss and other comprehensive income of the                企業之損益及其他全面收益。
          associate and joint venture. When the Group’s share of                  當本集團應佔聯營公司及合營
          losses of an associate and a joint venture exceeds the                  企業之虧損超出其於該聯營公
          Group’s interest in that associate or joint venture (which              司 或 合 營 企 業 之 權 益（ 即 包 括
          includes any long-term interests that, in substance, form               任何實質上構成本集團於該聯
          part of the Group’s net investment in the associate or                  營公司或合營企業之投資淨額
          joint venture), the Group discontinues recognising its                  之 長 期 權 益 ）， 則 本 集 團 不 再
          share of further losses. Additional losses are recognised               確認其應佔之進一步虧損。僅
          only to the extent that the Group has incurred legal or                 會於本集團已產生法定或推定
          constructive obligations or made payments on behalf of                  責任或替該聯營公司或合營企
          the associate or joint venture.                                         業支付款項之情況下，方會確
                                                                                  認額外虧損。


          An investment in an associate and a joint venture is                    聯營公司及合營企業投資於當
          accounted for using the equity method from the date on                  被投資方成為聯營公司或合營
          which the investee becomes an associate or a joint                      企業當日採用權益法入賬。
          venture. On acquisition of the investment in an associate               收購聯營公司或合營企業投資
          or a joint venture, any excess of the cost of the                       時，投資成本超出本集團應佔
          investment over the Group’s share of the net fair value of              被投資方可識別資產及負債淨
          the identifiable assets and liabilities of the investee is              公允值之任何數額乃確認為商
          recognised as goodwill, which is included within the                    譽，並包括在該投資的賬面值
          carrying amount of the investment. Any excess of the                    中。經重估本集團應佔可識別
          Group’s share of the net fair value of the identifiable                 資產及負債淨公允值超出投資
          assets and liabilities over the cost of the investment, after           成本之任何數額則立即在收購
          reassessment, is recognised immediately in profit or loss               該投資之期間在損益確認。
          in the period in which the investment is acquired.

          of distinct goods or services that are substantially the             上相同之一系列不同的貨品或
          same.                                                                服務。

          satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one           控制權隨時間的推移而轉移及
          of the following criteria is met:                                    收入隨時間的推移而確認：

                  the benefits provided by the Group’s performance                 集團執行本集團履約所提
                  as the Group performs;                                           供的效益；

                  asset that the customer controls as the Group                    一項資產，而該項資產於
                  performs; or                                                     本集團履約時由客戶控
                                                                                   制；或


          •       the Group’s performance does not create an asset             •   本集團的履約不會產生對
                  with an alternative use to the Group and the Group               本集團有其他用途的資產
                  has an enforceable right to payment for                          及本集團對迄今完成的履
                  performance completed to date.                                   約付款有可執行之權利。


          Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when             否則，當客戶取得不同貨品或
          the customer obtains control of the distinct good or                 服務的控制權時按一個時間點
          service.                                                             確認收入。

          Group has received consideration (or an amount of                    本集團有責任去轉移貨品或服

          Leases                                                               租賃
          Definition of a lease                                                租賃之定義
          A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys          當合約傳達權利以控制使用一
          the right to control the use of an identified asset for a            項已識別資產一段期間以換取
          period of time in exchange for consideration.                        代價，則該合約乃（或包含）一
                                                                               項租賃。


          For contracts entered into or modified on or after the               於 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號
          date of initial application of HKFRS 16 or arising from              初始應用日期或之後訂立或修
          business combinations, the Group assesses whether a                  改或由業務合併引起之合約，
          contract is or contains a lease based on the definition              本集團根據香港財務報告準則
          under HKFRS 16 at inception, modification date or                    第16號之定義於初始時、修改
          acquisition date, as appropriate. Such contract will not be          日期或收購日期，倘適用，評
          reassessed unless the terms and conditions of the                    估一份合約是否（或包含）一項
          contract are subsequently changed.                                   租賃。除非合約之條款及條件
                                                                               其後改變，此合約將不會再評
                                                                               估。


          The Group as a lessee                                                本集團作為承租人
          Short-term leases                                                    短期租賃
          The Group applies the short-term lease recognition                   本集團對租約期自開始日期計
          exemption that have a lease term of 12 months or less                12個月或更短者且並無包含購
          from the commencement date and do not contain a                      買權之租賃應用短期租賃確認
          purchase option. Lease payments on short-term leases                 豁免。短期租賃之租賃付款於
          are recognised as expense on a straight-line basis or                租約期內按直線法或其他系統
          another systematic basis over the lease term.                        基準確認為開支。

                received; and                                                       已收租賃補助；及


          •     any initial direct costs incurred by the Group.                 •   本集團涉及之任何初始直
                                                                                    接成本。


          Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any                    使用權資產以成本減去任何累
          accumulated depreciation and impairment losses, and                   計折舊及減值虧損，並就租賃
          adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liabilities.                  負債任何重新計量作出調整來
                                                                                計量。


          Right-of-use assets in which the Group is reasonably                  本集團合理確定於租賃期完結
          certain to obtain ownership of the underlying leased                  時可獲得相關租賃資產擁有權
          assets at the end of the lease term are depreciated from              之使用權資產，從開始日期至
          commencement date to the end of the useful life.                      可使用年期完結計算折舊。否
          Otherwise, right-of-use assets are depreciated on a                   則，使用權資產按其估計可使
          straight-line basis over the shorter of its estimated useful          用年期及租賃年期較短者，以
          life and the lease term.                                              直線法計算折舊。


          The Group presents right-of-use assets that do not meet               本集團將不符合投資物業定義
          the definition of investment property as a separate line              之使用權資產於綜合財務狀況
          item on the consolidated statement of financial position.             表以獨立項目列示。符合投資
          The right-of-use assets that meet the definition of                   物業定義之使用權資產則併入
          investment property are presented within “investment                  「投資物業」列示。
          properties”.

          Group uses the incremental borrowing rate at the lease                利率未能明顯確定，本集團會

                 circumstances of the particular contract.                         況。

          separate lease, the Group remeasures the lease liability             賃而言，本集團按修改租賃之

          discounting the revised lease payments using a revised               折現率將修訂租賃付款折現來
          discount rate at the effective date of the modification.             重新計量租賃負債。

          liabilities by making corresponding adjustments to the               相關使用權資產作出對應調整
          relevant right-of-use asset.                                         來入賬。

          and arranging an operating lease are added to the                     賃涉及的初始直接成本乃加至
          carrying amount of the leased asset, and such costs are               租賃資產之賬面值及該等成本
          recognised as an expense on a straight-line basis over the            以直線法於租賃期內確認為開
          lease term.                                                           支。

          Adjustments to fair value at initial recognition are                  按公允值初始計量。對初始確

                                                                                人之額外租賃付款。

          Foreign currencies                                                    外幣
          In preparing the financial statements of each individual              於編製各個別集團實體之財務
          group entity, transactions in currencies other than the               報表時，以該實體功能貨幣以
          functional currency of that entity (foreign currencies) are           外之貨幣（外幣）進行之交易均
          recognised at the rates of exchanges prevailing on the                按交易日期之適用換算匯率確
          dates of the transactions. At the end of the reporting                認。於報告期末，以外幣定值
          period, monetary items denominated in foreign                         之貨幣項目均按該日之適用匯
          currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing at that           率重新換算。非貨幣項目按外
          date. Non-monetary items that are measured in terms of                幣過往成本計量而毋須重新換
          historical cost in a foreign currency are not retranslated.           算。


          Exchange differences arising on the settlement of and on              結算及重新換算貨幣項目所產
          the retranslation of monetary items, and on the                       生之匯兌差額於其產生期間於
          retranslation of monetary items, are recognised in profit             損益內確認，惟源自其結算並
          or loss in the period in which they arise, except for                 無計劃及不大可能出現的應收
          exchange differences on monetary items receivable from                或應付境外業務之貨幣項目的
          or payable to a foreign operation for which settlement is             匯 兌 差 額（ 因 此 構 成 境 外 業 務
          neither planned nor likely to occur (therefore forming                淨 投 資 之 一 部 分 ）除 外 ， 該 差
          part of the net investment in the foreign operation),                 額初始於其他全面收益表確認
          which are recognised initially in other comprehensive                 及當出售或部分出售本集團於
          income and reclassified from equity to profit or loss on              聯營公司╱合營企業之權益時
          disposal or partial disposal of the Group’s interests in              重新由權益分類至損益。
          associates/joint ventures.


          For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial             就呈列綜合財務報表而言，本
          statements, the assets and liabilities of the Group’s                 集團經營業務之資產及負債乃
          operations are translated into the presentation currency              採用各報告期末當日之匯率換
          of the Group (i.e. HK$) using exchange rates prevailing at            算 為 本 集 團 之 列 賬 貨 幣（ 即 港
          the end of each reporting period. Income and expenses                 元 ）， 而 收 入 及 支 出 項 目 乃 按
          items are translated at the average exchange rates for the            該期間之平均匯率進行換算，
          period, unless exchange rates fluctuate significantly                 除非匯率於該期間內出現大幅
          during the period, in which case, the exchange rates at               波動則作別論，於此情況下，
          the date of transactions are used. Exchange differences               則採用於交易當日之匯率。所
          arising, if any, are recognised in other comprehensive                產生之匯兌差額（如有）乃於其
          income and accumulated in equity under the heading of                 他全面收益確認及於匯兌儲備
          translation reserve (attributed to non-controlling interests          項 下 權 益 累 計（ 適 用 時 歸 入 非
          as appropriate).                                                      控股權益）。

          accumulated in equity in respect of that operation                    產 ）， 本 公 司 擁 有 人 應 佔 與 該
          attributable to the owners of the Company are                         業務有關的所有於權益內累

                                                                                益。


          Borrowing costs                                                       借貸成本
          Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,             與購置、建造或生產合資格資
          construction or production of qualifying assets, which are            產（ 需 一 段 長 時 間 方 可 作 擬 定
          assets that necessarily take a substantial period of time to          用 途 或 可 供 銷 售 的 資 產 ）直 接
          get ready for their intended use or sale, are added to the            有關之借貸成本均加至該等資

          substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                   可作其擬定用途或可供銷售為
                                                                                止。


          Any specific borrowing that remain outstanding after the              於相關資產可用於擬定用途或
          related asset is ready for its intended use or sale is                出售後仍未償還的任何特定借
          included in the general borrowing pool for calculation of             貸計入一般借貸池，以計算一
          capitalisation rate on general borrowings. Investment                 般借貸的資本化率。特定借貸
          income earned on the temporary investment of specific                 在用於合資格資產之前的臨時
          borrowings pending their expenditure on qualifying assets             投資所賺取之投資收入從合資
          is deducted from the borrowing costs eligible for                     格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。
          capitalisation.


          All other borrowing costs are recognised in profit or loss            所有其他借貸成本均於產生期
          in the period in which they are incurred.                             間於損益中確認。

          Government grants                                                   政府補助
          Government grants are not recognised until there is                 政府補助於合理保證本集團將
          reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the            遵守其附帶的條件以及將收到
          conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be            補助的情況下方會予以確認。
          received.


          Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on a             政府補助於本集團將補助擬補
          systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                償的相關費用確認為費用的期
          recognises as expenses the related costs for which the              間內，以有系統的方式在損益
          grants are intended to compensate. Specifically,                    中確認。具體而言，政府補助
          government grants whose primary condition is that the               的主要條件為本集團應購買、
          Group should purchase, construct or otherwise acquire               建造或以其他方式收購非流動
          non-current assets are recognised as deferred income in             資產，其於綜合財務狀況表中
          the consolidated statement of financial position and                確認為遞延收入，並於相關資
          transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and rational          產的可使用年期以有系統及合
          basis over the useful lives of the related assets.                  理方式轉入損益。


          Government grants related to income that are receivable             與收入有關的政府補助，乃對
          as compensation for expenses or losses already incurred             已產生的費用或虧損，或向本
          or for the purpose of giving immediate financial support            集團提供即時的財政支持而
          to the Group with no future related costs are recognised            並無未來的相關成本之應收補
          in profit or loss in the period in which they become                償，於變為應收期間在損益中
          receivable. Such grants are presented under “other                  確 認 。 有 關 補 助 呈 列 於「 其 他
          income”.                                                            收入」項下。

          Retirement benefit costs                                              退休福利成本

          another HKFRS requires or permits the inclusion of the                務報告準則規定或准許將福利

          A liability is recognised for benefits accruing to employees          於扣除任何已支付的金額後，
          (such as wages and salaries, annual leave and sick leave)             對 應 付 予 僱 員 的 福 利（ 如 工 資
          after deducting any amount already paid.                              及 薪 金 、 年 假 及 病 假 ）確 認 負
                                                                                債。

          Taxation                                                               稅項
          Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax                       所得稅支出指現時應付稅項及
          currently payable and deferred tax.                                    遞延稅項的總和。


          The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for               現時應付稅項乃按年度應課稅
          the year. Taxable profit differs from ‘profit before tax’              盈利計算。應課稅盈利與除稅
          because of items of income or expense that are taxable                 前盈利不同乃源於其他年度應
          or deductible in other years and items that are never                  課稅或可扣稅的收入或開支項
          taxable or deductible. The Group’s current tax is                      目及從未課稅及扣稅的項目。
          calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or                   本集團之本期稅項乃按報告期
          substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.              末已實行或大致上已實行之稅
                                                                                 率計算。


          Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                    遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表
          between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in              之資產及負債賬面值與計算應
          the consolidated financial statements and the                          課稅盈利所採用相應稅基的暫
          corresponding tax bases used in the computation of                     時差額而確認。遞延稅項負債
          taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                 一般就所有應課稅暫時差額予
          recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred             以確認。遞延稅項資產一般就
          tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible                 所有可扣稅暫時差額確認，惟
          temporary differences to the extent that it is probable                僅於可能有應課稅盈利將可以
          that taxable profits will be available against which those             使用該可扣稅暫時差額時確
          deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such                 認。倘暫時差額乃由初始確認
          deferred assets and liabilities are not recognised if the              一項不影響應課稅盈利或會計
          temporary difference arises from the initial recognition               盈 利 的 交 易 的 資 產 及 負 債（ 於
          (other than in a business combination) of assets and                   業 務 合 併 中 除 外 ）所 引 起 ， 則
          liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable          有關遞延資產及負債不予確
          profit nor the accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax            認。此外，倘暫時差額源自初
          liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference             始確認商譽，則不確認遞延稅
          arises from the initial recognition of goodwill.                       項負債。

     3.2 Significant accounting policies (Continued)                              3.2 主要會計政策

          Taxation (Continued)                                                       （續）
                                                                                    稅項
          Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable                       遞延稅項負債一般就與附屬公
          temporary differences associated with investments in                      司之投資及合營企業權益而引
          subsidiaries and interest in a joint venture, except where                致之應課稅暫時差額而確認，
          the Group is able to control the reversal of the temporary                惟若本集團可控制暫時差額之
          difference and it is probable that the temporary                          撥回及暫時差額有可能不會於
          difference will not reverse in the foreseeable future.                    可見將來撥回之情況則除外。
          Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary                     與該等投資及權益相關的可扣
          differences associated with such investments and interest                 稅暫時差額所產生的遞延稅項
          are only recognised to the extent that it is probable that                資產，僅在可能有足夠應課稅
          there will be sufficient taxable profits against which to                 盈利可以使用暫時差額之效益
          utilise the benefits of the temporary differences and they                且預計於可見將來可以撥回時
          are expected to reverse in the foreseeable future.                        確認。


          The carrying amount of deferred tax assets is reviewed at                 遞延稅項資產之賬面值於各報
          the end of the reporting period and reduced to the                        告期末作檢討，並在沒可能會
          extent that it is no longer probable that sufficient taxable              有足夠應課稅盈利以收回全部
          profits will be available to allow all or part of the asset to            或部分資產時加以遞減。
          be recovered.


          Deferred tax assets and liabilities are measured at the tax               遞延稅項資產及負債按預期負
          rates that are expected to apply in the period in which the               債清償或資產變現期間內應用
          liability is settled or the asset is realised, based on tax rate          之稅率，以報告期末已生效或
          (and tax laws) that have been enacted or substantively                    實際上已生效之稅率（及稅法）
          enacted by the end of the reporting period.                               為基準計量。


          The measurement of deferred tax liabilities and assets                    遞延稅項負債及資產之計量反
          reflects the tax consequences that would follow from the                  映本集團於報告期末所預期對
          manner in which the Group expects, at the end of the                      收回或償還其資產及負債之
          reporting period, to recover or settle the carrying amount                賬面值之方式所產生的稅務後
          of its assets and liabilities.                                            果。

          to the right-of-use assets or the lease liabilities.                   產或租賃負債。

          Excess of depreciation on right-of-use assets over the                 估。使用權資產折舊超過租賃

          resulting in net deductible temporary differences.                     致可扣減暫時差額淨額。

          same taxation authority.                                               延稅項資產及負債會被抵銷。


          Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss,             本期及遞延稅項於損益確認，
          except when they relate to items that are recognised in                惟倘稅項涉及於其他全面收益
          other comprehensive income or directly in equity, in                   或直接於權益確認之項目，在
          which case, the current and deferred tax are also                      此情況下，本期及遞延稅項亦
          recognised in other comprehensive income or directly in                會分別於其他全面收益或直接
          equity respectively. Where current tax or deferred tax                 於權益內確認。倘本期稅項或
          arises from the initial accounting for a business                      遞延稅項由業務合併的初始會
          combination, the tax effect is included in the accounting              計產生，則稅項影響將包括於
          for the business combination.                                          業務合併的會計入賬。

          of the relevant asset is estimated in order to determine             估計相關資產的可收回金額，

          estimates the recoverable amount of the cash-generating              資產所屬現金產生單位的可收
          unit to which the asset belongs.                                     回金額。


          In testing a cash-generating unit for impairment,                    於測試現金產生單位作減值

          allocated to the smallest group of cash generating units             將分配給可以建立合理和一致
          for which a reasonable and consistent allocation basis               的最小現金產生單元組。企業
          can be established. The recoverable amount is determined             資產所屬現金產生單位或現金

          units to which the corporate asset belongs, and is                   額，並與相關現金產生單位或
          compared with the carrying amount of the relevant cash-              現金產生單元組之賬面值進行

          Inventories                                                             存貨
          Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net                     存貨按成本及可變現淨值兩者
          realisable value. Costs of inventories are determined on a              中之較低者列賬。存貨成本按
          first-in, first-out basis. Net realisable value represents the          先入先出基準釐定。可變現淨
          estimated selling price for inventories less all estimated              值指存貨估計售價減估計完成

          sales. Cost necessary to make the sale include incremental              之成本。進行銷售之所需成本
          costs directly attributable to the sale and non-incremental             包括銷售直接應佔之增量成本
          costs which the Group must incur to make the sale.                      及本集團為進行銷售而必須承
                                                                                  擔之非增量成本。


          Financial instruments                                                   金融工具

          when a group entity becomes a party to the contractual                  體成為工具訂約條文之訂約方
          provisions of the instrument. All regular way purchases or              時確認。所有常規方式買賣金
          sales of financial assets are recognised and derecognised               融資產按貿易日期基礎確認及
          on a trade date basis. Regular way purchases or sales are               剔除確認。常規方式買賣指要
          purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery            求按市場之法規或慣例設立之
          of assets within the time frame established by regulation               期限內交付資產的金融資產的
          or convention in the market place.                                      買賣。

          Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially                 金融資產及金融負債乃按公允
          measured at fair value except for trade receivables arising              值作初始計量，惟來自客戶合
          from contracts with customers which are initially                        約而產生的應收貿易賬款則根
          measured in accordance with HKFRS 15. Transaction                        據香港財務報告準則第15號作
          costs that are directly attributable to the acquisition or               初始計量。因收購或發行金融
          issue of financial assets and financial liabilities (other than          資 產 及 金 融 負 債（ 按 公 允 值 計
          financial assets and financial liabilities at fair value                 入損益（「按公允值計入損益」）
          through profit or loss (“FVTPL”)) are added to or                        之金融資產及金融負債除外）
          deducted from the fair value of the financial assets or                  而直接應佔之交易成本於初始
          financial liabilities, as appropriate, on initial recognition.           確認時，按適用情況加入或扣
          Transaction costs directly attributable to the acquisition               自該項金融資產或金融負債之
          of financial assets or financial liabilities at FVTPL are                公允值。因收購按公允值計入
          recognised immediately in profit or loss.                                損益之金融資產及金融負債而
                                                                                   直接應佔之交易成本即時於損
                                                                                   益確認。


          The effective interest method is a method of calculating                 實際利率法是一種計算金融資
          the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability           產或金融負債攤銷成本及於相
          and of allocating interest income and interest expense                   關期間分配利息收入及利息支
          over the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the             出的方法。實際利率是以精確
          rate that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts               將金融資產或金融負債預測可
          and payments (including all fees and points paid or                      使用年期或（如適用）更短期間
          received that form an integral part of the effective                     估 計 未 來 現 金 收 入 及 付 款（ 包
          interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or                   括所有已付或已收並構成實際
          discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset              利率整體之費用及點子、交易
          or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter                 成 本 及 其 他 溢 價 或 折 讓 ）折 現
          period, to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.               至初始確認時之賬面淨值之比
                                                                                   率。

          FVTPL.                                                                    值計入損益計量。

                                                                                         式；或


          •        it is a derivative that is not designated and effective           •   其為並非指定及有效對沖

          Impairment of financial assets                                          金融資產減值
          The Group performs impairment assessment under                          本集團於金融資產就預期信貸
          expected credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets                  虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進
          which are subject to impairment assessment under HKFRS                  行減值評估乃視乎香港財務準
          9 (including trade and other receivables, loan to a joint               則第9號項下的減值評估（包括
          venture, amounts due from related companies and bank                    應收貿易及其他賬款、合營企
          balances). The amount of ECL is updated at each                         業貸款、應收關連公司款項及
          reporting date to reflect changes in credit risk since initial          銀 行 結 餘 ）。 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 金
          recognition.                                                            額於每個報告日期更新以反映
                                                                                  自初始確認以來之信貸風險變
                                                                                  動。


          Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all               終身預期信貸虧損指由所有可
          possible default events over the expected life of the                   能違約事件超出相關工具預期
          relevant instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m                    壽命將引致之預期信貸虧損。
          ECL”) represents the portion of lifetime ECL that is                    相 反 ， 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損
          expected to result from default events that are possible                （「 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 」）指
          within 12 months after the reporting date. Assessment is                終身預期信貸虧損預期從可能
          done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                        於報告日期後12個月內的違約
          experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the               事件引致之部分。所作評估基
          debtors, general economic conditions and an assessment                  於本集團過往信貸虧損記錄，
          of both the current conditions at the reporting date as                 並調整債務人的特定因素，普
          well as the forecast of future conditions.                              遍經濟狀況及同時對於報告日
                                                                                  期之現況及預測未來狀況之評
                                                                                  估。

          The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for trade                      本集團通常就應收貿易賬款確
          receivables.                                                            認終身預期信貸虧損。


          For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                  就所有其他工具，本集團以相
          allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a                     等 於 12 個 月 預 期 信 貸 虧 損 計
          significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,          量虧損撥備，除非自初始確認
          in which case the Group recognises lifetime ECL. The                    後信貸風險顯著增加，本集團
          assessment of whether lifetime ECL should be recognised                 則確認終身預期信貸虧損。評
          is based on significant increases in the likelihood or risk             估是否應確認終身預期信貸虧
          of a default occurring since initial recognition.                       損乃基於自初始確認後發生違
                                                                                  約的可能性或風險是否顯著增
                                                                                  加。


          (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                               (i)   信貸風險顯著增加


                In assessing whether the credit risk has increased                      於評估自初始確認後信貸
                significantly since initial recognition, the Group                      風險是否顯著增加時，本
                compares the risk of a default occurring on the                         集團將於報告日期金融工
                financial instrument as at the reporting date with                      具發生違約之風險與於初
                the risk of a default occurring on the financial                        始確認日期金融工具發生
                instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In                    違約之風險作比較。作出
                making this assessment, the Group considers both                        評估時，本集團同時考慮
                quantitative and qualitative information that is                        合理及有理據的定量及定
                reasonable and supportable, including historical                        性資料，包括過往經驗及
                experience and forward-looking information that is                      無需花費成本或精力而可
                available without undue cost or effort.                                 得之前瞻性資料。

                have low credit risk if i) it has a low risk of default,                倘i）金融資產的違約風險
                ii) the borrower has a strong capacity to meet its                      屬低；ii）借款人具強勁能

                and iii) adverse changes in economic and business                       流 量 責 任 ； 及 iii ）長 遠 的

                necessarily, reduce the ability of the borrower to                      可能，但不一定，減低借
                fulfil its contractual cash flow obligations. The                       款人履行其合約現金流量
                Group considers a financial asset to have low credit                    責任的能力，則該金融資

                of ‘investment grade’ as per globally understood                        當金融資產於內部或外部

                 The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of                      本集團定期監控用作識別
                 the criteria used to identify whether there has been                   是否出現信貸風險顯著增
                 a significant increase in credit risk and revises them                 加的標準之有效性及當適
                 as appropriate to ensure that the criteria are                         用時修訂標準，以確保該
                 capable of identifying significant increase in credit                  標準於金額逾期前可識別
                 risk before the amount becomes past due.                               信貸風險顯著增加。


          (ii)   Definition of default                                           (ii)   違約的定義


                 For internal credit risk management, the Group                         就內部信貸風險管理，當
                 considers an event of default occurs when                              內部開發資料或取得外部
                 information developed internally or obtained from                      資料顯示債務人不太可能
                 external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely                 全數支付其債權人，包括
                 to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full                     本 集 團（ 並 未 考 慮 本 集 團
                 (without taking into account any collaterals held by                   持 有 任 何 抵 押 品 ）， 本 集
                 the Group).                                                            團認為發生違約事件。


                 Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that                    不管上述情況如何，當金
                 default has occurred when a financial asset is more                    融 資 產 逾 期 超 過 90 天 ，
                 than 90 days past due unless the Group has                             本集團認為已發生違約事
                 reasonable and supportable information to                              件，除非本集團具合理及
                 demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is                   有理據資料顯示滯後違約
                 more appropriate.                                                      標準更為合適。

          (iii)   Credit-impaired financial assets                                 (iii)   信貸減值金融資產


                  A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or                         當一項或多項違約事件發
                  more events of default that have a detrimental                           生令金融資產估計未來現
                  impact on the estimated future cash flows of that                        金流量具不利影響，該金
                  financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a                           融資產則信貸減值。金融
                  financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable                   資產信貸減值的證據包括
                  data about the following events:                                         以下事件之可觀察數據：


                  (a)   significant financial difficulty of the issuer or                  (a)   發行人或借款人存
                        the borrower;                                                            在嚴重財政困難；


                  (b)   a breach of contract, such as a default or past                    (b)   違約，即如違約或
                        due event;                                                               過期事件；


                  (c)   the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic                        (c)   借款人之貸款人，
                        or contractual reasons relating to the                                   就有關借款人財政
                        borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted                          困難之經濟或合約
                        to the borrower a concession(s) that the                                 原因，給予借款人
                        lender(s) would not otherwise consider; or                               一般貸款人不會考
                                                                                                 慮的讓步；或


                  (d)   it is becoming probable that the borrower                          (d)   借款人有可能面臨
                        will enter bankruptcy or other financial                                 破產或其他財務重
                        reorganisation.                                                          組。

          (iv)   Write-off policy                                               (iv)   撇銷政策


                 The Group writes off a financial asset when there is                  當有資料顯示對手方存在
                 information indicating that the counterparty is in                    嚴重財政困難及並無實際
                 severe financial difficulty and there is no realistic                 把握收回款項，例如，當
                 prospect of recovery, for example, when the                           對手方已進行清盤或已
                 counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                     進入破產程序，或如為
                 has entered into bankruptcy proceedings, or in the                    應收貿易賬款，當金額乃
                 case of trade receivables, when the amounts are                       逾期超過兩年，以較早者
                 over two years past due, whichever occurs sooner.                     為準，本集團撇銷金融資
                 Financial assets written off may still be subject to                  產。金融資產的撇銷可能
                 enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery                     仍須根據本集團回收程序
                 procedures, taking into account legal advice where                    的執行方式，當適用時考
                 appropriate. A write-off constitutes a derecognition                  慮法律建議。撇銷構成剔
                 event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised in                    除確認事項。任何其後回
                 profit or loss.                                                       撥於損益確認。

      3.2 Significant accounting policies (Continued)                         3.2 主要會計政策
                                                                                       （續）

          Financial instruments (Continued)                                        （續）
                                                                                金融工具
          Financial assets (Continued)                                          金融資產（續）
          Impairment of financial assets (Continued)                                 （續）
                                                                                金融資產減值
          (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                              (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確
                                                                                      認


                The measurement of ECL is a function of the                           預期信貸虧損計量具違約
                probability of default, loss given default (i.e. the                  可 能 性 ， 默 認 虧 損（ 即 違
                magnitude of the loss if there is a default) and the                  約 的 損 失 幅 度 ）及 違 約 曝
                exposure at default. The assessment of the                            光的功能。違約可能性及
                probability of default and loss given default is based                默認虧損的評估乃基於過
                on historical data and forward-looking information.                   往數據及前瞻性資料。預
                Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and                            期信貸虧損的估計反映不
                probability-weighted amount that is determined                        偏倚及概率加權金額乃按
                with the respective risks of default occurring as the                 發生違約相關風險的比重
                weights.                                                              來釐定。


                Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                      一般而言，預期信貸虧損
                contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                   為根據合約所有應付本集
                accordance with the contract and the cash flows                       團合約現金流量與本集團
                that the Group expects to receive, discounted at                      於初始確認時預期收到，
                the effective interest rate determined at initial                     按實際利率折現的現金流
                recognition.                                                          量的差額。

                                                                                   （續）
                                                                                   認


                Where ECL is measured on a collective basis, the                   當預期信貸虧損按集體基
                financial instruments are grouped on the following                 準計量，金融工具將按以
                basis:                                                             下基準組合：


                •        Past-due status;                                          •    逾期狀況；


                •        Nature, size and industry of debtors;                     •    債務人之性質、規
                                                                                        模及行業；


                •        Internal credit ratings; and                              •    內部信貸評級；及


                •        External credit ratings where available.                  •    當適用時之外部信
                                                                                        貸評級。


                The grouping is regularly reviewed by management                   管理層定期審視組別情況
                to ensure the constituents of each group continue                  以確保每一組別要素繼續
                to share similar credit risk characteristics.                      具備類似的信貸風險特
                                                                                   徵。


                Interest income is calculated based on the gross                   利息收入按金融資產賬面
                carrying amount of the financial asset unless the                  總值計算，而已作信貸減
                financial asset is credit impaired, in which case                  值之金融資產則除外，在
                interest income is calculated based on amortised                   此情況下，利息收入則按
                cost of the financial asset.                                       金融資產之攤銷成本計
                                                                                   算。


                The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in                 本集團就所有金融工具經
                profit or loss for all financial instruments by                    調整其賬面值於損益賬確
                adjusting their carrying amount, with the exception                認減值收益或虧損，而應
                of trade receivables where the corresponding                       收貿易賬款則除外，其相
                adjustment is recognised through a loss allowance                  關調整乃通過虧損撥備賬
                account.                                                           確認。

          another entity. If the Group retains substantially all the           另一實體，本集團方會剔除確

          proceeds received.                                                   融資產並就已收取款項確認抵
                                                                               押借貸。

          Company are recognised at the proceeds received, net of               益工具按收取所得款項減直接

          amount indefinitely are classified as equity instruments.             金，被分類為權益工具。

          instruments.                                                          虧。

19. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES                                                     19. 投資物業

      The Group leases out commercial and industrial properties                 本集團根據經營租賃按每月付租出
      under operating leases with rentals payable monthly. The leases           租商業及工業物業。租賃通常初始
      typically run for an initial period of 1 to 10 years, with unilateral     年期為1至10年，承租人單方面有權
      rights to extend the lease beyond initial period held by lessees          延續租賃至初始年期外。大部分租
      only. Majority of the lease contracts contain annual lease                賃合約在租期內固定年度租賃付款。
      payment that are fixed over the lease term.


      The Group is not exposed to foreign currency risk as a result of          本集團在租賃安排並無承受外幣風
      the lease arrangements, as all leases are denominated in the              險，因所有租賃乃按集團實體相關
      respective functional currencies of group entities. The lease             功能貨幣計值。租賃合約並無包含
      contracts do not contain residual value guarantee and lessee’s            剩餘價值保證及承租人於租期完結
      option to purchase the property at the end of lease term.                 時購入物業之權利。

32. RETIREMENT BENEFITS PLANS                                          32. 退休福利計劃

      Defined contribution scheme                                        界定供款計劃

      The Group operates a MPF Scheme for all qualifying employees       本集團在香港為所有合資格僱員設
      in Hong Kong. The assets of the MPF scheme are held                立一項強積金計劃。強積金計劃
      separately from those of the Group, in funds under the control     的資產與本集團的資產分開持有，
      of trustees. The Group contributes monthly the lower of            以及撥入信託人管理的基金。本集
      HK$1,500 or 5% of the relevant monthly payroll costs to the        團 按 1,500 港 元 或 有 關 月 薪 成 本 的
      MPF Scheme, which contribution is matched by employees.            5%，以較低者為準，向強積金計劃
                                                                         供款，與僱員之供款一致。


      The only obligation of the Group with respect of MPF Scheme is     本集團對強積金計劃的唯一責任就
      to make the required contribution payable on the future years.     是在未來年度作出所需應付供款。
      There are no forfeited contributions for the MPF Scheme as the     由於供款於向強積金計劃付款後全
      contributions are fully vested to the employees upon payments      數歸屬予僱員，故強積金計劃並無
      to MPF Scheme.                                                     沒收供款。


      The employees employed in the PRC subsidiaries are members         中國附屬公司聘用的僱員均參與中
      of the state-managed retirement benefit scheme operated by         國政府營辦的國家管理退休福利
      the PRC government. The PRC subsidiaries are required to           計劃。中國附屬公司須按僱員薪金
      contribute a certain percentage of their payroll to the            的若干百分比，向退休福利計劃供
      retirement benefit scheme to fund the benefits. The only           款，作為福利之用。本集團對退休
      obligation of the Group with respect to the retirement benefit     福利計劃的唯一責任就是作出指定
      scheme is to make the specified contributions.                     供款。

35. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                35. 資本風險管理

      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the           本集團管理其資本以確保本集團的
      Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                實體可持續經營業務，並透過最佳
      maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation         債務及權益比例為權益持有人提供
      of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy           最高回報。本集團的整體政策維持
      remains unchanged from prior year.                                     與去年一致。


      The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which            本集團的資本結構包括債務，當中
      include the bank borrowings and lease liabilities as disclosed in      包括分別於附註30及28披露之銀行
      notes 30 and 28 respectively, and equity attributable to equity        借貸及租賃負債，以及本公司權益
      holders of the Company, comprising issued share capital,               持有人應佔權益（包括已發行股本、
      various reserves and perpetual capital securities.                     多項儲備及永續資本證券）。

          Credit risk and impairment assessment                             信貸風險及減值評估
          Credit risk refers to the risk that the Group’s                   信貸風險指本集團交易對手方
          counterparties default on their contractual obligations           之合約責任違約導致本集團蒙
          resulting in financial losses to the Group. The Group’s           受財務損失之風險。本集團
          credit risk exposures are primarily attributable to trade         之信貸風險主要來自應收貿易
          and other receivables, amounts due from related                   及其他款項、應收關連公司款
          companies, bank balances and loan to a joint venture.             項、銀行結餘及合營企業貸
          Save as certain trade receivables disclosed in note 24(a)         款。除附註24(a)披露之若干應
          and trade receivables backed by bills, the Group does not         收貿易款項及有票據質押之應
          hold any collateral or other credit enhancements to cover         收貿易賬款外，本集團並無持
          its credit risks associated with its financial assets.            有任何抵押品或其他信用增強
                                                                            措施以覆蓋與其金融資產相關
                                                                            之信貸風險。


          In order to minimise the credit risk, management of the           為盡量減低信貸風險，本集團
          Group has delegated a team responsible for                        管理層已委任一組人員釐定信
          determination of credit limits, credit approvals and other        貸限額、信貸審批以及其他監
          monitoring procedures to ensure that follow-up action is          控程序，確保就收回逾期債項
          taken to recover overdue debts. In addition, the Group            採取跟進行動。此外，本集團
          performs impairment assessment under ECL model under              在應用香港財務報告準則第9
          application of HKFRS 9 on trade balances individually or          號根據預期信貸虧損模式對貿
          based on collective assessment. In this regard, the               易結餘個別地或集體進行減值
          directors consider that the Group’s credit risk is                評估。就此，董事認為本集團
          significantly reduced.                                            之信貸風險已大大減少。


          The credit risk on bank balances and trade receivables            由於交易方均為信譽評級良好
          backed by bills is limited because the counterparties are         及國際信用評級機構給予高信

          assigned by international credit-rating agencies.                 有票據質押之應收貿易賬款之
                                                                            信貸風險有限。


          Trade receivables consist of a large number of customers,         本集團的應收貿易賬款包括大
